<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0"><span style="font-weight: bold;">Warrant Liabilities</span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: justify">&#160;</p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: justify"> The Company accounts for Public Warrants as liabilities
under ASC 815-40, recognized at fair value upon issuance with subsequent changes recognized in the statement of operations per ASC 815-40-35-4.
The Public Warrants are classified as liabilities because the Warrant Agreement contains a provision under which warrant holders may receive
cash in connection with a tender offer if such tender offer is accepted by holders of more than <ix:nonFraction name="crac:PercentageOfReceiveCashInConnectionWithTenderOfferAcceptedOrdinaryShareholders" contextRef="cref_1993224015" unitRef="uref_593216447" scale="-2" decimals="2" format="ixt:num-dot-decimal" id="ixv-6413">50</ix:nonFraction>%
of Class A ordinary shares &#8212; an event outside the sole control of the Company. Fair value is estimated using a 500-step Binomial
Lattice model (see Note 5). </p></ix:nonNumeric>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0"><span style="font-weight: bold;">&#160;</span></p><ix:nonNumeric name="crac:PublicRightsPolicyTextBlock" contextRef="cref_368472693" escape="true" id="ixv-3691">

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0"><span style="font-weight: bold;">Public Rights</span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: justify">&#160;</p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: justify">The Company accounts for Public Rights as permanent
equity under ASC 815-40. Each Right entitles the holder to receive 0.20 of a Class A ordinary share upon consummation of a Business Combination
for no additional consideration. Rights are indexed to the Company&#8217;s own stock, contain no net-cash settlement provisions, and expire
worthless in liquidation. The fair value of Rights at issuance is recorded within additional paid-in capital (see Note 6). Rights are
not remeasured subsequent to initial recognition.</p></ix:nonNumeric>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0"><span style="font-weight: bold;">&#160;</span></p><ix:nonNumeric name="us-gaap:EarningsPerSharePolicyTextBlock" contextRef="cref_368472693" escape="true" id="ixv-3712">

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0"><span style="font-weight: bold;">Net Income Per Ordinary Share</span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: justify">&#160;</p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: justify"> The Company applies the two-class method under
ASC 260 to compute earnings per ordinary share. Both Class A and Class B ordinary shares are participating securities sharing equally
in dividends and distributions. Net income is allocated between classes based on weighted-average shares outstanding. Accretion of Class
A shares to redemption value is a direct equity charge excluded from the EPS numerator. Diluted EPS equals basic EPS as there are <span style="-sec-ix-hidden:fc_2014446780">no</span>
dilutive securities. </p></ix:nonNumeric>

of offering costs were paid by the Sponsor on the Company&#8217;s behalf after closing and allocated to Class A temporary equity, for
total offering costs charged to equity of $<ix:nonFraction name="us-gaap:AdjustmentsToAdditionalPaidInCapitalStockIssuedIssuanceCosts" contextRef="cref_608564830" unitRef="uref_1993224015" scale="0" decimals="0" format="ixt:num-dot-decimal" id="ixv-6432">2,552,133</ix:nonFraction>.
All transaction costs were allocated to temporary equity (Class A ordinary shares). </p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: justify">&#160;</p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: justify"> Simultaneously with the IPO closing, the Sponsor
purchased <ix:nonFraction name="crac:StockIssuedDuringPeriodOptionsToPurchaseUnits" contextRef="cref_812030492" unitRef="uref_1828230851" scale="0" decimals="0" format="ixt:num-dot-decimal" id="ixv-6433">175,000</ix:nonFraction>
Private Placement Units and Polaris Advisory Partners purchased <ix:nonFraction name="crac:StockIssuedDuringPeriodOptionsToPurchaseUnits" contextRef="cref_1872962517" unitRef="uref_1828230851" scale="0" decimals="0" format="ixt:num-dot-decimal" id="ixv-6434">200,000</ix:nonFraction>
Private Placement Units at $<ix:nonFraction name="us-gaap:SharesIssuedPricePerShare" contextRef="cref_940128387" unitRef="uref_1061706265" scale="0" decimals="2" format="ixt:num-dot-decimal" id="ixv-6435">8.00</ix:nonFraction>
per unit, generating $<ix:nonFraction name="us-gaap:ProceedsFromIssuanceOfPrivatePlacement" contextRef="cref_812030492" unitRef="uref_1993224015" scale="0" decimals="0" format="ixt:num-dot-decimal" id="ixv-6436">3,000,000</ix:nonFraction>,
which was deposited into the Trust Account by the Sponsor. </p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: justify">&#160;</p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: justify"> Each Unit consists of <ix:nonFraction name="crac:NumberOfSharesIssuedPerUnit" contextRef="cref_165811882" unitRef="uref_1828230851" scale="0" decimals="0" format="ixt-sec:numwordsen" id="ixv-6437">one</ix:nonFraction>
Class A ordinary share, one-half of <ix:nonFraction name="crac:NumberOfWarrantsIssuedPerUnit" contextRef="cref_537700514" unitRef="uref_1828230851" scale="0" decimals="0" format="ixt-sec:numwordsen" id="ixv-6438">one</ix:nonFraction>
redeemable warrant (&#8220;Public Warrant&#8221;), and one right (&#8220;Public Right&#8221;). Upon separation, each whole Public Warrant
entitles the holder to purchase one Class A ordinary share at an exercise price of $<ix:nonFraction name="us-gaap:SaleOfStockPricePerShare" contextRef="cref_165811882" unitRef="uref_1061706265" scale="0" decimals="2" format="ixt:num-dot-decimal" id="ixv-6439">11.50</ix:nonFraction>
per share, and each Public Right entitles the holder to receive 0.20 of a Class A ordinary share upon consummation of a Business Combination.
Units began separate trading on Nasdaq on or about December 9, 2025 under the symbols CRAC, CRACW, and CRACR. </p></ix:nonNumeric>

4 &#8212; RELATED PARTY TRANSACTIONS</span></span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0"><span style="font-weight: bold;">Founder Shares</span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: justify">&#160;</p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: justify"> On April 29, 2025, the Sponsor received <ix:nonFraction name="us-gaap:StockIssuedDuringPeriodSharesNewIssues" contextRef="cref_873027816" unitRef="uref_1828230851" scale="0" decimals="0" format="ixt:num-dot-decimal" id="ixv-6440">4,312,500</ix:nonFraction>
Class B ordinary shares for aggregate consideration of $<ix:nonFraction name="us-gaap:StockIssuedDuringPeriodValueNewIssues" contextRef="cref_873027816" unitRef="uref_1993224015" scale="0" decimals="0" format="ixt:num-dot-decimal" id="ixv-6441">25,000</ix:nonFraction>
(approximately $<ix:nonFraction name="us-gaap:SharePrice" contextRef="cref_1901035244" unitRef="uref_1061706265" scale="0" decimals="3" format="ixt:num-dot-decimal" id="ixv-6442">0.006</ix:nonFraction>
per share). The over-allotment option was exercised in full at IPO closing; accordingly, <span style="-sec-ix-hidden:fc_44293832">no</span>
Founder Shares were subject to forfeiture as of December 31, 2025. The Founder Shares will automatically convert into Class A ordinary
shares upon consummation of a Business Combination on a one-for-one basis, subject to certain adjustments. </p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0"><span style="font-weight: bold;">&#160;</span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0"><span style="font-weight: bold;">Sponsor Funding of Company Operations</span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: justify">&#160;</p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: justify"> The Company held no operating cash during the
period. The Sponsor paid all formation and operating costs totaling $<ix:nonFraction name="us-gaap:OperatingCostsAndExpenses" contextRef="cref_260984415" unitRef="uref_1993224015" scale="0" decimals="0" format="ixt:num-dot-decimal" id="ixv-6443">126,601</ix:nonFraction>
on the Company&#8217;s behalf, consisting of: (i) $<ix:nonFraction name="us-gaap:AccountsPayableAndAccruedLiabilitiesCurrent" contextRef="cref_498003060" unitRef="uref_1993224015" scale="0" decimals="0" format="ixt:num-dot-decimal" id="ixv-6444">101,667</ix:nonFraction>
accrued in accounts payable and accrued expenses on the balance sheet; and (ii) $<ix:nonFraction name="us-gaap:OperatingCostsAndExpenses" contextRef="cref_1993224015" unitRef="uref_1993224015" scale="0" decimals="0" format="ixt:num-dot-decimal" id="ixv-6445">24,934</ix:nonFraction>
of operating costs paid directly by the Sponsor with no corresponding balance sheet entry (reflected as a non-cash activity in the statement
of cash flows). Additionally, the Sponsor paid $<ix:nonFraction name="us-gaap:NoninterestExpenseOfferingCost" contextRef="cref_260984415" unitRef="uref_1993224015" scale="0" decimals="0" format="ixt:num-dot-decimal" id="ixv-6446">2,308,385</ix:nonFraction>
of offering costs on behalf of the Company (reflected as a non-cash activity in the statement of cash flows), consisting of $<ix:nonFraction name="us-gaap:NoninterestExpenseOfferingCost" contextRef="cref_624999705" unitRef="uref_1993224015" scale="0" decimals="0" format="ixt:num-dot-decimal" id="ixv-6447">2,079,000</ix:nonFraction>
in offering costs channeled through the Sponsor&#8217;s account at the IPO closing and $<ix:nonFraction name="us-gaap:NoninterestExpenseOfferingCost" contextRef="cref_1595748729" unitRef="uref_1993224015" scale="0" decimals="0" format="ixt:num-dot-decimal" id="ixv-6448">229,385</ix:nonFraction>
of direct post-IPO and post-9/30 offering cost payments to legal counsel, auditors, and the transfer agent. </p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0"><span style="font-weight: bold;">&#160;</span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0"><span style="font-weight: bold;">Due from Related Party</span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: justify">&#160;</p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: justify"> As of December 31, 2025, the Company had a receivable
of $<ix:nonFraction name="us-gaap:OtherReceivablesNetCurrent" id="fc_494114313" contextRef="cref_494114313" unitRef="uref_1993224015" scale="0" decimals="0" format="ixt:num-dot-decimal">448,082</ix:nonFraction>
due from the Sponsor. This amount represents Private Placement proceeds received by the Sponsor on the Company&#8217;s behalf that were
designated for use outside the Trust Account, net of formation and operating costs paid by the Sponsor on the Company&#8217;s behalf and
other amounts owed to the Sponsor. The net amount is classified as a current asset on the balance sheet. The amounts are non-interest
bearing, unsecured and due on demand. </p></ix:nonNumeric>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0"><span style="text-transform: uppercase"><span style="font-weight: bold;">&#160;</span></span></p> <ix:nonNumeric name="us-gaap:DerivativeInstrumentsAndHedgingActivitiesDisclosureTextBlock" contextRef="cref_368472693" escape="true" id="ixv-3781">

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0"><span style="text-transform: uppercase"><span style="font-weight: bold;">NOTE
5 &#8212; WARRANT LIABILITIES</span></span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: justify">&#160;</p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: justify"> The Company issued <ix:nonFraction name="us-gaap:StockIssuedDuringPeriodSharesNewIssues" id="fc_1965883822" contextRef="cref_1965883822" unitRef="uref_1828230851" scale="0" decimals="0" format="ixt:num-dot-decimal">8,625,000</ix:nonFraction>
redeemable warrants (&#8220;Public Warrants&#8221;) as part of the IPO Units (each Unit contained one-half of one Public Warrant). Each
whole Public Warrant entitles the holder to purchase one Class A ordinary share at an exercise price of $<ix:nonFraction name="us-gaap:ClassOfWarrantOrRightExercisePriceOfWarrantsOrRights1" contextRef="cref_1381127300" unitRef="uref_1061706265" scale="0" decimals="2" format="ixt:num-dot-decimal" id="ixv-6451">11.50</ix:nonFraction>
per share, subject to adjustment. The Public Warrants will become exercisable <ix:nonNumeric name="crac:PeriodOfWarrantsAfterPublicOffering" contextRef="cref_418250140" format="ixt-sec:durday" id="ixv-6452">30</ix:nonNumeric>
days after the completion of a Business Combination and will expire <ix:nonNumeric name="crac:ExpirationOfWarrantsTerm" contextRef="cref_1993224015" id="ixv-6453">five
years</ix:nonNumeric> after the completion of a Business Combination, or earlier upon redemption or liquidation. </p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: justify">&#160;</p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: justify"> The Company may redeem the outstanding Public
Warrants at a price of $<ix:nonFraction name="us-gaap:ClassOfWarrantOrRightExercisePriceOfWarrantsOrRights1" contextRef="cref_1510673747" unitRef="uref_1061706265" scale="0" decimals="2" format="ixt:num-dot-decimal" id="ixv-6454">0.01</ix:nonFraction>
per warrant if the Class A ordinary share price equals or exceeds $<ix:nonFraction name="us-gaap:SharePrice" contextRef="cref_1809718416" unitRef="uref_1061706265" scale="0" decimals="2" format="ixt:num-dot-decimal" id="ixv-6455">18.00</ix:nonFraction>
per share for any <ix:nonNumeric name="crac:MinimumTradingDaysForMeetingSharePriceCondition" contextRef="cref_539261072" format="ixt-sec:durday" id="ixv-6456">20</ix:nonNumeric>
trading days within a <ix:nonNumeric name="crac:MeasurementPeriodForConsideringSharePriceCondition" contextRef="cref_539261072" format="ixt-sec:durday" id="ixv-6457">30</ix:nonNumeric>-trading
day period commencing at least <ix:nonNumeric name="crac:MaximumPeriodForFilingAfterBusinessCombination" contextRef="cref_539261072" format="ixt-sec:durday" id="ixv-6458">150</ix:nonNumeric>
days after a Business Combination. </p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: justify">&#160;</p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: justify"> The Public Warrants are classified as liabilities
at fair value in accordance with ASC 815-40. The Company determined that the Public Warrants do not meet the criteria for equity classification
because the Warrant Agreement contains a provision pursuant to which the warrant holders could receive cash in connection with a tender
offer accepted by more than <ix:nonFraction name="crac:PercentageOfReceiveCashInConnectionWithTenderOfferAcceptedOrdinaryShareholders" contextRef="cref_142584847" unitRef="uref_593216447" scale="-2" decimals="2" format="ixt:num-dot-decimal" id="ixv-6459">50</ix:nonFraction>%
of the Class A ordinary shareholders &#8212; an event that is outside the Company&#8217;s control. Accordingly, the Public Warrants are
recorded as liabilities at fair value, with changes in fair value recognized in the statement of operations each reporting period. </p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0">&#160;</p><ix:exclude>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0"></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0"><span style="font-weight: bold;">Fair Value Measurement</span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: justify">&#160;</p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: justify"> The Public Warrants are classified as Level 3
in the fair value hierarchy as they are valued using a binomial lattice model with unobservable inputs. The significant inputs used in
the valuation were: stock price of $<ix:nonFraction name="us-gaap:WarrantsAndRightsOutstandingMeasurementInput" contextRef="cref_2022424038" unitRef="uref_593216447" scale="0" decimals="2" format="ixt:num-dot-decimal" id="ixv-6460">10.00</ix:nonFraction>,
strike price of $<ix:nonFraction name="us-gaap:WarrantsAndRightsOutstandingMeasurementInput" contextRef="cref_1457288511" unitRef="uref_593216447" scale="0" decimals="2" format="ixt:num-dot-decimal" id="ixv-6461">11.50</ix:nonFraction>,
expected volatility of <ix:nonFraction name="us-gaap:WarrantsAndRightsOutstandingMeasurementInput" contextRef="cref_1500691288" unitRef="uref_593216447" scale="-2" decimals="3" format="ixt:num-dot-decimal" id="ixv-6462">12.3</ix:nonFraction>%,
and a risk-free rate of <ix:nonFraction name="us-gaap:WarrantsAndRightsOutstandingMeasurementInput" contextRef="cref_505407007" unitRef="uref_593216447" scale="-2" decimals="4" format="ixt:num-dot-decimal" id="ixv-6463">3.52</ix:nonFraction>%
at issuance (<ix:nonFraction name="us-gaap:SharePrice" id="fc_1510673747" contextRef="cref_1510673747" unitRef="uref_1061706265" scale="0" decimals="2" format="ixt:num-dot-decimal">3.48</ix:nonFraction>%
at December 31, 2025). The remaining contractual term was <ix:nonFraction name="us-gaap:WarrantsAndRightsOutstandingMeasurementInput" contextRef="cref_900661321" unitRef="uref_593216447" scale="0" decimals="2" format="ixt:num-dot-decimal" id="ixv-6465">1.25</ix:nonFraction>
years at issuance and approximately <ix:nonFraction name="us-gaap:WarrantsAndRightsOutstandingMeasurementInput" contextRef="cref_952247912" unitRef="uref_593216447" scale="0" decimals="2" format="ixt:num-dot-decimal" id="ixv-6466">1.11</ix:nonFraction>
years at December 31, 2025. </p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0"><span style="text-transform: uppercase"><span style="font-weight: bold;">&#160;</span></span></p> <ix:nonNumeric name="crac:PublicRightsTextBlock" contextRef="cref_368472693" escape="true" id="ixv-3906">

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0"><span style="text-transform: uppercase"><span style="font-weight: bold;">NOTE
6 &#8212; PUBLIC RIGHTS</span></span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: justify">&#160;</p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: justify"> The Company issued <ix:nonFraction name="crac:UnitsIssuedDuringPeriodSharesNewIssues" id="fc_973730354" contextRef="cref_973730354" unitRef="uref_1828230851" scale="0" decimals="0" format="ixt:num-dot-decimal">17,250,000</ix:nonFraction>
Public Rights as part of the IPO Units. Each Public Right automatically entitles the holder to receive one-fifth (1/5) of one Class A
ordinary share upon the consummation of a Business Combination, for no additional consideration. No fractional shares will be issued upon
exchange of the Public Rights; fractional entitlements will be rounded down. If the Company does not complete a Business Combination within
the Combination Period, the Public Rights will expire worthless. </p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: justify">&#160;</p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: justify"> The Public Rights are classified as permanent
equity under ASC 815-40 because they are indexed to the Company&#8217;s own stock and do not contain any net-cash settlement provisions.
Accordingly, <ix:nonFraction name="crac:UnitsOutstandingDuringPeriodShareIssues" id="fc_1186896812" contextRef="cref_1993224015" unitRef="uref_1828230851" scale="0" decimals="0" format="ixt:num-dot-decimal">17,250,000</ix:nonFraction>
outstanding Public Rights would result in the issuance of <ix:nonFraction name="crac:UnitsIssuedDuringPeriodSharesNewIssues" id="fc_631107141" contextRef="cref_142584847" unitRef="uref_1828230851" scale="0" decimals="0" format="ixt:num-dot-decimal">3,450,000</ix:nonFraction>
additional Class A ordinary shares upon consummation of a Business Combination. The Public Rights are not remeasured after initial recognition.
</p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: justify">&#160;</p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: justify"> The Company measured the fair value of the Public
Rights at the IPO date using a probability-weighted expected return method (PWERM) based on a de-SPAC probability estimate of <ix:nonFraction name="crac:ConversionRatioPercentage" contextRef="cref_1993224015" unitRef="uref_593216447" scale="-2" decimals="3" format="ixt:num-dot-decimal" id="ixv-6478">25.0</ix:nonFraction>%,
a conversion ratio of 0.20, and a risk-free rate of <ix:nonFraction name="crac:PublicRightsMeasurementInput" id="fc_779956041" contextRef="cref_779956041" unitRef="uref_593216447" scale="0" decimals="2" format="ixt:num-dot-decimal">3.52</ix:nonFraction>%
over an estimated remaining term of <ix:nonFraction name="crac:PublicRightsMeasurementInput" id="fc_1614257894" contextRef="cref_1614257894" unitRef="uref_593216447" scale="0" decimals="2" format="ixt:num-dot-decimal">1.25</ix:nonFraction>
years, resulting in a fair value of $0.4778 per right. The aggregate fair value of $<ix:nonFraction name="us-gaap:AdjustmentsToAdditionalPaidInCapitalMarkToMarket" contextRef="cref_1595748729" unitRef="uref_1993224015" scale="0" decimals="0" format="ixt:num-dot-decimal" id="ixv-6481">8,242,050</ix:nonFraction>
(<ix:nonFraction name="crac:UnitsIssuedDuringPeriodSharesNewIssues" contextRef="cref_1595748729" unitRef="uref_1828230851" scale="0" decimals="0" format="ixt:num-dot-decimal" id="ixv-6482">17,250,000</ix:nonFraction>
&#215; $<ix:nonFraction name="crac:AdjustmentsToAdditionalPaidInFairValue" contextRef="cref_1595748729" unitRef="uref_1061706265" scale="0" decimals="4" format="ixt:num-dot-decimal" id="ixv-6483">0.4778</ix:nonFraction>)
was recorded as an increase to additional paid-in capital at the IPO date. </p></ix:nonNumeric>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0">&#160;</p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0"></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0"><span style="text-transform: uppercase"><span style="font-weight: bold;">NOTE
7 &#8212; CLASS A ORDINARY SHARES SUBJECT TO POSSIBLE REDEMPTION</span></span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: justify">&#160;</p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: justify"> All <ix:nonFraction name="us-gaap:TemporaryEquitySharesOutstanding" contextRef="cref_1809718416" unitRef="uref_1828230851" scale="0" decimals="0" format="ixt:num-dot-decimal" id="ixv-6484">17,250,000</ix:nonFraction>
Class A ordinary shares are classified as temporary equity (&#8220;Class A ordinary shares subject to possible redemption&#8221;) in accordance
with ASC 480-10-S99-3A. Redemption is considered probable given the governing documents&#8217; mandatory redemption provisions upon expiration
of the Combination Period. The Company accretes the carrying value of Class A ordinary shares to their redemption value at each reporting
date, with accretion charged first to additional paid-in capital until exhausted, and the remainder charged to accumulated deficit. </p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: justify">&#160;</p><ix:nonNumeric name="us-gaap:TemporaryEquityTableTextBlock" contextRef="cref_368472693" escape="true" id="ixv-3932">

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0"><span style="text-transform: uppercase"><span style="font-weight: bold;">NOTE
8 &#8212; COMMITMENTS AND CONTINGENCIES</span></span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0"><span style="font-weight: bold;">&#160;</span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0"><span style="font-weight: bold;">Deferred Underwriting Fee</span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: justify">&#160;</p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: justify"> The Company is obligated to pay Polaris Advisory
Partners a deferred underwriting commission of $<ix:nonFraction name="crac:UnderwritersDiscountPayableUponInitialPublicOffering" contextRef="cref_1993224015" unitRef="uref_1993224015" scale="0" decimals="0" format="ixt:num-dot-decimal" id="ixv-6503">300,000</ix:nonFraction>,
which will become payable from the Trust Account solely upon the completion of a Business Combination. The deferred underwriting commission
is recorded as a liability on the Company&#8217;s balance sheet. If the Company does not complete a Business Combination within the Combination
Period and liquidates, the deferred underwriting commission will be forfeited and Polaris Advisory Partners will not receive any portion
of the deferred fee. </p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0"><span style="font-weight: bold;">&#160;</span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0"><span style="font-weight: bold;">Registration and Shareholder Rights</span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: justify">&#160;</p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: justify">The holders of the Founder Shares, Private Placement
Units, and any shares that may be issued upon conversion of working capital loans (and all underlying securities) are entitled to registration
rights pursuant to a Registration and Shareholder Rights Agreement dated November 7, 2025, requiring the Company to register such securities
for resale. The holders of these securities will be entitled to make up to three demands, excluding short form registration demands, that
the Company register such securities. In addition, the holders have certain &#8220;piggy-back&#8221; registration rights with respect
to registration statements filed subsequent to completion of a Business Combination and rights to require the Company to register for
resale such securities pursuant to Rule 415 under the Securities Act. However, the Registration and Shareholder Rights Agreement provides
that the Company will not be required to effect or permit any registration or cause any registration statement to become effective until
the securities covered thereby are released from their lock-up restrictions. The Company will bear the expenses incurred in connection
with the filing of any such registration statements.</p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0">&#160;</p><ix:exclude>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0"><span style="text-transform: uppercase"><span style="font-weight: bold;">NOTE
10 &#8212; NET INCOME PER ORDINARY SHARE &#8212; TWO-CLASS METHOD</span></span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: justify">&#160;</p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: justify">The Company complies with the accounting and disclosure
requirements of ASC 260, &#8220;Earnings Per Share.&#8221; Net income per ordinary share is computed by dividing net income by the weighted-average
number of ordinary shares outstanding during the period. The Company applies the two-class method in calculating net income per ordinary
share, as both Class A and Class B ordinary shares participate equally in dividends and other distributions. The accretion of Class A
ordinary shares to redemption value is excluded from earnings per share as it is a direct equity transaction.</p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: justify">&#160;</p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: justify">The following table reflects the calculation of
basic and diluted net income per ordinary share for the period from April 29, 2025 (inception) to December 31, 2025:</p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: justify">&#160;</p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; text-align: justify; margin: 0pt 0"><span style="text-transform: uppercase"><span style="font-weight: bold;"><span id="a_014" style="display: inline-block;"></span>Item

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: justify">&#160;</p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: justify">None.</p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0"><span style="text-transform: uppercase"><span style="font-weight: bold;"><span id="a_015" style="display: inline-block;"></span>Item
9A. Controls and Procedures</span></span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0">&#160;</p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0"><span style="font-weight: bold;">Evaluation of Disclosure Controls
and Procedures</span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: justify">&#160;</p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: justify">Our management, with the participation of our
Chief Executive Officer and Chief Financial Officer, evaluated the effectiveness of the Company&#8217;s disclosure controls and procedures,
as defined in Rules 13a-15(e) and 15d-15(e) under the Exchange Act, as of December 31, 2025. Based on that evaluation, our Chief Executive
Officer and Chief Financial Officer concluded that, as of December 31, 2025, the Company&#8217;s disclosure controls and procedures were
not effective due to the material weakness described below.</p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0"><span style="font-weight: bold;">&#160;</span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0"><span style="font-weight: bold;">Material Weakness</span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: justify">&#160;</p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: justify">Management identified a material weakness in internal
control over financial reporting in connection with the accounting for complex financial instruments &#8212; specifically, the initial
classification and valuation of warrant liabilities and the valuation of Public Rights.</p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0"><span style="font-weight: bold;">&#160;</span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0"><span style="font-weight: bold;">Management&#8217;s Annual Report on
Internal Control Over Financial Reporting</span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: justify">&#160;</p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: justify">This Annual Report on Form 10-K does not include
an attestation report of our independent registered public accounting firm regarding internal control over financial reporting pursuant
to Section 404(b) of the Sarbanes-Oxley Act. We are an emerging growth company, as defined in the JOBS Act, and as such we are exempt
from the attestation requirement.</p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: justify">&#160;</p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: justify">Management is responsible for establishing and
maintaining adequate internal control over financial reporting as defined in Rules 13a-15(f) and 15d-15(f) under the Exchange Act. Management
conducted an evaluation of the effectiveness of our internal control over financial reporting based on the criteria set forth in Internal
Control &#8212; Integrated Framework issued by the Committee of Sponsoring Organizations of the Treadway Commission (2013 framework).
Based on this evaluation, management concluded that our internal control over financial reporting was not effective as of December 31,
2025 due to the material weakness described above.</p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0"><span style="font-weight: bold;">&#160;</span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0"><span style="font-weight: bold;">Changes in Internal Control Over Financial
Reporting</span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: justify">&#160;</p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: justify">Other than the remediation activities described
above, there have been no changes in our internal control over financial reporting during the quarter ended December 31, 2025 that have
materially affected, or are reasonably likely to materially affect, our internal control over financial reporting.</p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: justify">&#160;</p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0"><span style="text-transform: uppercase"><span style="font-weight: bold;"><span id="a_025" style="display: inline-block;"></span>Item

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: justify">&#160;</p>

or <ix:nonNumeric name="ecd:Rule10b51ArrTrmntdFlag" contextRef="cref_368472693" format="ixt:fixed-false" id="ixv-6538"><ix:nonNumeric name="ecd:NonRule10b51ArrTrmntdFlag" contextRef="cref_368472693" format="ixt:fixed-false" id="ixv-6539">terminated</ix:nonNumeric></ix:nonNumeric>
any contract, instruction or written plan for the purchase or sale of securities of the registrant intended to satisfy the affirmative
defense conditions of Rule 10b5-1(c), or any &#8220;non-Rule 10b5-1 trading arrangement&#8221; as defined in paragraph (c) of Item 408
of Regulation S-K, during the quarter ended December 31, 2025. </p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: justify">&#160;</p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0"><span style="text-transform: uppercase"><span style="font-weight: bold;"><span id="a_016" style="display: inline-block;"></span>Item
9C. Disclosure Regarding Foreign Jurisdictions that Prevent Inspections</span></span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: justify">&#160;</p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: justify">Not applicable.</p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: justify">&#160;</p>
